      "id": "tfc:steel",
      "label": "Steel",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.7,
      "support": 15,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:crafting/metal/anvil/steel",
          "confidence": 0.65
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "confidence": 0.7
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Steel is an advanced material which can be used to create tools and Armor, and comes in a few different varieties: Steel, Black Steel, Red Steel, and Blue Steel. In order to create steel, you must first create Pig Iron in a Blast Furnace, and cast it into Ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "A Pig Iron Ingot can then be worked in an anvil to create a High Carbon Steel ingot, which can be worked again to create a Steel Ingot",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Black Steel is an advanced form of steel formed from an alloy of steel and some other metals. In order to create it, you will need to create an alloy called Weak Steel in a Crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Black Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Weak Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 50 - 70 % : Steel - 15 - 25 % : Nickel - 15 - 25 % : Black Bronze Molten Weak Steel can be cast into ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Weak Steel Ingots can then be welded with Pig Iron Ingots to create High Carbon Black Steel Ingots. Finally, these can be worked on an Anvil to create Black Steel Ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Black Steel can be used to craft tools and Armor, and also is used as a key ingredient in the creation of Blue Steel and Red Steel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,

          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Blue Steel is one of the two highest tier metals, along with Red Steel. Similar to Black Steel, the first step is to create an alloy of Weak Blue Steel in a Crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Blue Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Weak Blue Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 50 - 55 % : Black Steel - 20 - 25 % : Steel - 10 - 15 % : Bismuth Bronze - 10 - 15 % : Sterling Silver",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Weak Blue Steel Ingots can be welded with Black Steel Ingots to create High Carbon Blue Steel Ingots. Finally, these can be worked on an Anvil to create Blue Steel Ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Blue Steel can be used to create tools and Armor, and a Blue Steel Bucket, which can be used to transport 1000 mB of both liquid fluids, and Lava.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Red Steel is one of the two highest tier metals, along with Blue Steel. Similar to Black Steel, the first step is to create an alloy of Weak Red Steel in a Crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Red Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Weak Red Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.title",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 50 - 55 % : Black Steel - 20 - 25 % : Steel - 10 - 15 % : Brass - 10 - 15 % : Rose Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Weak Red Steel Ingots can be welded with Black Steel Ingots to create High Carbon Red Steel Ingots. Finally, these can be worked on an Anvil to create Red Steel Ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Red Steel can be used to create tools and Armor, and a Red Steel Bucket, which can be used to transport 1000 mB of both liquid fluids, and Lava",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Sensor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "The oxygen sensor is a block that will emit a redstone signal when it detects oxygen. You can shift-right-click it to invert the signal. Despite its name, you can also change its detection type by right-clicking with a wrench. It has the option to detect oxygen, safe temperature, and normal gravity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer.json",
          "text": "The energizer is a machine capable of powering items and storing large amounts of energy. To charge an item, power the energizer and then right-click on the energizer with that item. It stores 2 million energy, making it capable of charging machines for a very long time. It also retains its energy when broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Energizer",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags.json",
          "text": "Flags are decorative blocks available in 16 colors. By default, when placed, they will display the face of the flag owner. They can be configured to display a custom image from a URL by right-clicking and entering the URL in the GUI. This will display the internet image onto the flag.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flags",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid pipes are similar to cables but with fluid transfer instead of energy transfer. To use them, craft some fluid pipes and a wrench. Then, right-click on one of the pipes with the wrench to cycle through the pipe's modes: 'none,' 'normal,' 'insert,' and 'extract.'",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Pipes with 'insert' set will receive fluid from the network, while pipes with 'extract' set will pump fluid into the network.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Like cables, fluid pipes also have a duct block for transfering fluids safely in and out of sealed structures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps.json",
          "text": "Industrial Lamps",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Industrial Lamps",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps.json",
          "text": "Industrial Lamps",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Industrial Lamps",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps.json",
          "text": "Industrial lamps are available in all 16 colors, and in small and large sizes. By default, these will all emit normal white light; however, if you have Shimmer, each light will emit its respective color.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Industrial Lamps",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit.json",
          "text": "Netherite Space Suit",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Netherite Space Suit",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit.json",
          "text": "Netherite Space Suit",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Netherite Space Suit",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit.json",
          "text": "The netherite space suit is an upgraded version of the standard space suit, featuring permanent fire resistance, more protection and the ability to survive on Venus and Mercury. Before you travel to these planets, you must craft the netherite space suit armour or else you will burn to death.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Netherite Space Suit",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors.json",
          "text": "Sliding Doors",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Sliding Doors",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors.json",
          "text": "Sliding Doors",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Sliding Doors",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors.json",
          "text": "Sliding doors are large 3x3 doors. They seal oxygen when closed, making them great candidates for airlocks. They can be locked by shift-right-clicking them with a wrench. Locked doors can only be opened with redstone.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Sliding Doors",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/trophies",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/trophies.json",
          "text": "Globes are trophy blocks found as a reward at the end of a dungeon. Globes offer no practical function and are instead a decoration block. They can be spun around by right-clicking or can spin forever when powered by redstone. Only the Moon globe is obtainable in survival at the moment.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Trophies",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/trophies",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/trophies.json",
          "text": "Space Paintings",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Trophies",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/trophies",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/trophies.json",
          "text": "Space paintings are found in chests throughout dungeons. They work exactly like regular paintings but only place paintings from this mod. There are 14 pieces to look for.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Trophies",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {

          "text": "Venus and Mercury",
          "key": "guide-page:name",

          "count": 2

        {

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury.json",
          "text": "Once you have enough Ostrum to craft a complete netherite space suit set and all of the components of the tier three rocket, you can travel to Venus and Mercury.           "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",

          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury.json",
          "text": "Calorite is used to craft the ultimate rocket capable of travelling to a new solar system.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Venus and Mercury",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates.json",
          "text": "Creating Plates",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Creating Plates",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates.json",
          "text": "Once you've got energy, you'll want to craft a compressor. This machine can compress ingots and blocks into plates, which are needed to craft many things.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Creating Plates",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Even after you place your rocket, you will not be able to fly just yet. To fly, you will need to fill the rocket with fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Oil is found in large amounts spouting over the ocean surface. Collect as much oil as you can, as oil is non-renewable, and you need a lot of it for each rocket launch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "After collecting enough oil (at least six buckets for a launch and return), you will need to refine the oil into fuel. To do this, you will need to craft a fuel refinery. Power it, then place the oil in the left slot, and it will be refined into fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel Refinery",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Shift-click on the rocket to open its inventory, then place three buckets of fuel into the input slot. Place the other three buckets into the rocket's inventory for a return trip.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Generating Energy",
          "key": "guide-page:name",

          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Coal Generator",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Energy is core resource of Ad Astra. It's used in all machines and is required for everything. There are multiple ways to generate energy, the most basic way being the coal generator. It uses burnable resources like wood or coal to generate energy.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "The world of TerraFirmaCraft is divided into large continents - landmasses many kilometers wide and separated by oceans. In these, you may find mountain ranges, rivers, and many other environments.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "A typical TFC world viewed at a large scale.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "The world is also divided up into different types of Rock. Rock regions can be over a kilometer across, and there will usually be two or three different rock layers under your feet at all times. As different ores are found in different rock types, locating specific rock types can be very important for finding resources such as Ores, which will often only appear in certain rock types.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Rock Layers",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "The cross-section of a TFC world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Ocean floors are composed of igneous extrusive rock - rocks that are formed by magma which cools quickly. Underneath igneous extrusive rock will likely be a igneous intrusive rock of the same grade. For example, under Basalt (a mafic, igneous extrusive rock), will likely be Gabbro (a mafic, igneous intrusive rock).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Ocean Floors",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "- Felsic rocks are Granite and Rhyolite. - Intermediate rocks are Andesite, Dacite, and Diorite. - Mafic rocks are Basalt and Gabbro.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Igneous Rock Grades",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "The top layer of rock on a continent will either be igneous extrusive, or sedimentary. Underneath sedimentary rocks will likely be metamorphosed forms of the rock above. For example, Marble (a metamorphic rock) will likely be found under Limestone or Chalk. High grade metamorphic rocks are found deep under other metamorphic or igneous rocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "- Slate forms under Shale, Claystone, and Conglomerate. - Marble forms under Limestone, Dolomite, and Chalk. - Quartzite forms under Chert. - Phyllite forms under Slate - Schist and Gneiss form under Phyllite, or other igneous intrusive rocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Metamorphic Rocks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Finally, in mountainous regions you might also see uplift, where a metamorphic or igneous intrusive rock is found on the surface. Uplift rocks can be found above other continental sedimentary or higher-grade metamorphic rocks. In addition, Dikes - small vertical slices of igneous intrusive rock - may appear scattered around the world,",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Uplift Regions",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "protruding through the upper layers of rocks. With all that in mind, the next few pages list the rocks of all four categories: Sedimentary, Metamorphic, Igneous Extrusive, and Igneous Intrusive. These categories determine where the rock can spawn (see the previous pages), and also what ores may spawn in this rock.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Sedimentary rocks are formed by the accumulation or deposition of mineral or organic particles. They are typically found on the top layers of rock in continental areas. They are: - Shale - Claystone - Limestone - Conglomerate - Dolomite - Chert - Chalk",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Metamorphic rocks are created by a process called metamorphism. They can be found underneath corresponding sedimentary or igneous rock, or in uplift areas. They are: - Quartzite - Slate - Phyllite - Schist - Gneiss - Marble",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Igneous Extrusive rocks are formed from magma cooling on the Earth's surface. They can be found on the top layer of rock in continental areas, or on the floor of oceans. They are: - Rhyolite - Basalt - Andesite - Dacite",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Igneous Extrusive",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Igneous Intrusive rocks are formed from magma which cooled under the Earth's crust. They can be found deep underground, or rarely in dikes or uplift areas. They are: - Granite - Diorite - Gabbro",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Igneous Intrusive",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Building Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "The building gadget makes building a large number of blocks all at once extremely easy! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to build with.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Building Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Build to Me. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to place the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Build Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be built",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, building will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Don't forget you can undo if you make a mistake!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "The copy paste gadget makes replicating structures far easier! To start, ensure you're in 'copy' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a green outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copying a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. The block you right clicked during copying is the starting point of the structure. To place the structure in world, simply right click! All items necessary for building will be removed from your inventory, and any blocks you don't have items for will be skipped.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "To see a list of the items you need, open the settings menu and choose the 'material list' option. This is a scrollable list showing what items you do or do not have, and can be sorted in multiple ways. Try it out!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Material List",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Needed items",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Its possible to make fine tuned adjustments to the copy area by opening the Adjust Placement window in the settings menu. From here, you can adjust the position of the start block and end block. Click the plus and minus buttons to see the green box change in real time. You can also swap to 'absolute' coordinates mode and enter in-game world coordinates instead.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Copy",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the area you are copying",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Theres a similar option for paste mode. In this UI, you'll be adjusting the location of the starting block. Click the buttons and the render should update in-world in real time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the location of the paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The cut paste gadget makes moving structures a breeze, and works just like the Copy Paste Gadget! To start, ensure you're in 'cut' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a red outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cutting a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The structure will not be removed until you click the 'cut' button in the settings menu. Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. To place the structure in world, simply right click! No items are necessary for this, as its using the blocks you removed earlier.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "You can only have one structure stored on the gadget at a time. Placing it in the world will erased the stored information on the gadget. Be careful when pasting the structure, you only get one chance, exchange mode is recommended! If you attempt to cut a new area while data already exists, you'll be prompted to confirm. If you do this, the first set of data will be lost forever!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "All block-entity data will be moved along with the structure, including the contents of chests and block configurations. Much like the Copy Paste Gadget, you can modify the cut or paste positions with the appropriate settings menu option. See that chapter for details!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "The destruction gadget makes clearing a large area very easy. Any blocks cleared will be voided!. First, open the settings menu and adjust the area. This area is with respect to the block (and block face) you are looking at. If you look at the top face of a block, 'up' will be relative to the player's screen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destroying an area, click the arrow to see diffent pictures!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Once you're satisfied with the settings, right click on a block to void all the highlighted blocks. They will be removed from the world. You may undo this action if you make a mistake. It will not remove items from your inventory to undo, as the gadget is just returning the voided blocks to the world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "The exchanging gadget can swap one set of blocks for another! It cannot, however, replace air blocks. Use a Building Gadget for that! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to exchange to.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Exchanging Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Surface. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed over top of existing blocks. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to exchange the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchange Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be exchanged",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, exchanging will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Items for the removed blocks will be returned to you. Note: You can enchant this tool with silk touch. Without it grass will return dirt, etc.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can undo with the exchanging gadget, but you must have the necessary items. For example, if you exchanged grass into stone bricks, you must have grass in your inventory to undo this exchange. Don't forget to apply silk touch if desired!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor.json",
          "text": "Anchor allows you to 'lock in' the block you're looking at, so you can freely move or look around. There is a hotkey for this, or you can access it from the settings menu. You can right click at any time to build the structure when it is anchored. Note: Changing range may not take effect until you remove and replace the anchor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Anchor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor.json",
          "text": "Anchor your render!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Anchor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
    {
