        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "The Beneath hosts its own unique selection of wild fruits, which have adapted to the extreme conditions underground. Wild fruits can be cultivated in all the same ways as regular wild fruits in the Overworld.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "- Lavacado - Magmango",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 8 - 80 °C Hydration: 20 - 100 % Lavacado trees grow in the months of December through April, start flowering in May and June, and bear fruit in July.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "An example lavacado tree.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 8 - 80 °C Hydration: 30 - 100 % Magmango trees grow in the months of October through February, start flowering in March and April, and bear fruit in May.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "An example magmango tree.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "The fruiting calendar for fruit trees.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Fruit Trees",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft.json",
          "text": "If you see Tualang or Fig trees in your travels, you'll be pleased to know that you'll be able to create Boats with them just like any other kind of Wood",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "ArborFirmaCraft",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft.json",
          "text": "Softwoods have sufficiently soft enough pulp and bark that they're perfect for creating Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "ArborFirmaCraft",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft.json",
          "text": "- Tualang - Fig - Cypress",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "ArborFirmaCraft",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft.json",
          "text": "Known for their exceptional sturdiness, Hardwood trees are ideal for creating larger and sophisticated Watercrafts such as Rowboats and Sloops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "ArborFirmaCraft",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft.json",
          "text": "- Baobab - Eucalyptus - Mahogany - Hevea - Teak - Ironwood - Ipe",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "ArborFirmaCraft",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Martian Boats",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Way off on the far reaches of Mars, you can find both Crimson and Warped wood to create some rather flashy Boats.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Softwoods have sufficiently soft enough pulp and bark that they're perfect for creating Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "- Crimson",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Known for their exceptional sturdiness, Hardwood trees are ideal for creating larger and sophisticated Watercrafts such as Rowboats and Sloops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "- Warped",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Deep within the nether you can utilize both Crimson and Warped wood to create some rather flashy Boats. Format Error: Due to a limitation, Watercrafts created with these materials are NOT impervious to lava, you WILL sink and BURN.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Softwoods have sufficiently soft enough pulp and bark that they're perfect for creating Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "- Crimson",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Known for their exceptional sturdiness, Hardwood trees are ideal for creating larger and sophisticated Watercrafts such as Rowboats and Sloops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",

          "item_ref_count": 8,

        },
        {

          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",

          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Products",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "The most simple glass products are Glass Panes and Glass Blocks. In order to craft them, you must start with a Blowpipe with a Glass Batch, and then perform a Pour. - Table Pours are used to create Glass Panes - Basin Pours are used to create Glass Blocks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass can also be dyed before it is poured to create colored glass. The color is dependent on the type of glass batch, and any powders that have been added. Each type of Glass Batch has a different natural color of glass that they will create. Silica glass batches can be made into many colors, Olivine, and Volcanic glass can be made into relatively few colors.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Panes are made with a Table Pour. A pouring table is made by placing up to sixteen Kaolin Clay Blocks or Brass Plated Blocks in a continuous area. 1. Add a Glass Batch to a Blowpipe. 2. Heat the blowpipe to Faint Red. 3. the Blowpipe on the top of the table. 4. Finally with a Paddle to flatten the glass.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,

          "text": "Glass Blocks are made with a Basin Pour. A basin is made by surrounding all sides of an air block except the top with Kaolin Clay Blocks or Brass Plated Blocks. 1. Add a Glass Batch to a Blowpipe. 2. Heat the blowpipe to Faint Red. 3. the Blowpipe on the top of the table.",

          "text": "Glass has a natural color based on the type of Glass Batch that was used. Other colors can be made using a Bowl. To use, place the Bowl on the ground, then the required Powder. Before Pouring, use the Blowpipe on the bowl to add the powder to the batch.",
